if @Tid>0
	begin
	update tblTable_info set TableCode=@TableCode,Capcity=@Capcity,TCid=@TCid,RevStatus=@RevStatus,Description=@Decription,ModifiedBy=@CreatedBy,ModifiedOn=SYSDATETIME() ,BranchId =@BranchId
		,Status=@Status where Tid=@Tid 
		SET @TableId= @Tid
	end
	else
	begin
	INSERT INTO tblTable_info(TableCode,Capcity,TCid,RevStatus,Description,BranchId,CreatedBy,CreatedOn,Status)
	VALUES(@TableCode,@Capcity,@TCid,@RevStatus,@Decription,@BranchId,@CreatedBy,SYSDATETIME(),@Status) 
	Set @TableId=SCOPE_IDENTITY()
	end
SELECT @TableId  as Tid
RETURN 
End

GO

SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[prdInsert_upd_FoodCategory] 
@Fcid int, 
@CategoryName varchar(max),
@Description varchar(max),
@BranchId bigint,
@CreatedBy bigint,
@Status int
AS
Declare @fcatid bigint; 
Begin
if @fcatid>0
	begin
	update tblFoodCategory set CategoryName=@CategoryName,Description=@Description,ModifiedBy=@CreatedBy,ModifiedDateTime=SYSDATETIME() ,BranchId =@BranchId
		,Status=@Status where Fcid=@Fcid 
		SET @fcatid= @Fcid
	end
	else
	begin
	INSERT INTO tblFoodCategory(CategoryName,Description,BranchId,CreatedBy,CreatedDateTime,Status)
	VALUES(@CategoryName,@Description,@BranchId,@CreatedBy,SYSDATETIME(),@Status) 
	Set @fcatid=SCOPE_IDENTITY()
	end
SELECT @fcatid  as Fcid
RETURN 
End
	
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[prdInsert_upd_ItemReg]
@ItemId int, 
@Itemname varchar(max),
@summary varchar(max),
@Fcid bigint,
@price bigint,
@quantity bigint,
@unit bigint,
@BranchId bigint,
@CreatedBy bigint,
@Status int
AS
Declare @iid bigint; 
Begin
if @ItemId>0
	begin
	update tblItem set Itemname=@Itemname,summary=@summary,Fcid=@Fcid,price=@price,quantity=@quantity,unit=@unit,ModifiedBy=@CreatedBy,ModifiedOn=SYSDATETIME() ,BranchId =@BranchId
		,Status=@Status where ItemId=@ItemId 
		SET @iid= @ItemId
	end
	else
	begin
	INSERT INTO tblItem(Itemname,summary,Fcid,price,quantity,unit,BranchId,CreatedBy,CreatedOn,Status)
	VALUES(@Itemname,@summary,@Fcid,@price,@quantity,@unit,@BranchId,@CreatedBy,SYSDATETIME(),@Status) 
	Set @iid=SCOPE_IDENTITY()
	end
SELECT @iid  as ItemId
RETURN 
End
	
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[prdInsert_upd_menu]
@MId int, 
@Title varchar(max),
@Description varchar(max),
@FcategoryId bigint,
@BranchId bigint,
@CreatedBy bigint,
@Status int
AS
Declare @menuid bigint; 
Begin
if @MId>0
	begin
	update tblMenu set Title=@Title,Description=@Description,FcategoryId=@FcategoryId,ModifiedBy=@CreatedBy,ModifiedOn=SYSDATETIME() ,BranchId =@BranchId
		,Status=@Status where MId=@MId 
		SET @menuid= @MId
	end
	else
	begin
	INSERT INTO tblMenu(Title,Description,FcategoryId,BranchId,CreatedBy,CreatedOn,Status)
	VALUES(@Title,@Description,@FcategoryId,@BranchId,@CreatedBy,SYSDATETIME(),@Status) 
	Set @menuid=SCOPE_IDENTITY()
	end
SELECT @menuid  as MId
RETURN 
End

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[prdSearch]

@Select nvarchar(MAX),
@Table nvarchar(MAX),
@Where nvarchar(MAX),
@OrderBy nvarchar(MAX)
as
begin
execute('Select '+@Select+' from '+@Table +' '+ @Where +' ' +@OrderBy)
end
